[decision record](v9-meeting-decisions.md) line 45: "Restricting live access does not by itself establish Git object confidentiality". | N6: an upload-pack policy table beside the receive-pack table in §5.3, and an explicit choice between per-caller object-set isolation and declared repository-wide readability. N6 (P4.3a item 0) owns per-workspace serving and the S1 regression from the v8 review line 26; N9 (d)/(e) record the owner in §15 and make P8.8 verify it. |
| S2 (P1): same-origin preview executes with the viewer's platform origin | Mentioned without a mechanism. Owner-only relay access removes the v8 case of a reader or admin viewing an owner-controlled preview, but repository code still runs on the platform origin with the owner's full platform authority; subdomain mode stays optional and the design says the finding is open. | [design §6.8](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 631: relay "at `/api/v1/workspaces/:id/preview/:port/*` ... a workspace-scoped ticket cookie, and optional subdomain mode. ... Browser-origin and untrusted-content isolation still require the historical security review gates; owner-only access alone does not close those findings." [workspace-sharing](workspace-sharing-v9.md) line 58: "Only the current owner may start, replace or cancel it, inspect its live ports, or access its live relay." [plan P5.1](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 957: "Retain HTTP/WebSocket relay at /api/v1/workspaces/:id/preview/:port/* ... Tickets/streams bind current owner, authorization generation and VM incarnation". No P5.1 step, [plan §7.1](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) row (lines 1347 to 1364) or [plan P8.8](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) item (lines 1208 to 1224) names a preview origin. | N5: a mandatory isolated per-(workspace, port) preview origin for shared deployments, fail closed when unconfigured, ticket cookie only on the preview host, deletion of the same-origin legacy branch; added to P5.1 item 5 and a §7.1 row. N5 (P5.1 item 5) names the owning PR and the v8 review line 36 regression; N9 (d)/(e) record the owner. |
| S3 (P1): proxied module calls lack a distinct authenticated capability boundary | Mentioned without a mechanism. V9 keeps the v8 shape: one per-VM proxy secret handed to every launched process, `G5D_EXEC_ID` as a plain value, and "the execution identity carried in the request" named but not authenticated. A per-execution capability exists only for the native-reply channel. New v9 surfaces (result submission, replies) rely on the same unauthenticated execution identity. | [design §6.5](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 561: the secret is "passed only to the dispatcher (`G5D_LLM_PROXY_SECRET`), which includes it in the proxy configuration it gives every process it launches"; line 567: "`G5D_EXEC_ID` and `G5D_SESSION_ID` are plain values, not secrets ... the API still applies the incarnation fence and the execution identity carried in the request"; line 570: the dispatcher "does not pass it to the processes it launches; provider processes hold placeholders only". [design §11.1](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 956: "with `G5D_EXEC_ID` naming the execution; none holds a token". [design §5.9](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 448: submission requires "the designated producer's scoped current execution acting for the child owner". [design §6.7](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 605: the dispatcher "intersects the requested capabilities with the control-plane-issued `grant_id`" (guest-side, not at the proxy or API). [worker-host-api §6.5](worker-host-api-v9.md) line 243: "plain `G5D_EXEC_ID` and `G5D_SESSION_ID` correlation values; the proxy injects `g5_api` workspace credentials and the API checks current incarnation and execution". [plan P4.12](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 888 item 4: "the fence and the execution identity in the request stay the API's checks". [agent-api-surface §4.4](agent-api-surface-v9.md) line 167: "Workspaces validates the private per-execution control capability" for `modules.input.respond` only. | N3: two guest principals with a per-execution capability minted at `modules.invoke` or a dispatcher-owned local socket that stamps `exec_id` server-side; route classes the repository-process principal may not reach. N27: `token_use`/`aud` on the workspace token and the route set it admits. N75: the `exec_id` text encoding the API and `g5d` agree on. The design reserves publication for the API and defers the external read grant; the companion permits direct provider pushes; the grant shape carries only `target_host`. | [design §5.6](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 364: "Provider credentials never enter the VM. Private git dependencies on GitHub or GitLab are reached through the credential proxy with an org-level read-only grant (§6.5) or are unsupported." [design §17.2](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 1135: "Org-level read-only provider grants for private git dependencies, and the `gh` credential path with a per-VM CA" listed as deferred, no PR named. [design §6.3](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 533 and [worker-host-api §4](worker-host-api-v9.md) line 137: `{ kind: git_https, target_host, source: workspace_token | secret_ref }`, no repository or operation field. [worker-host-api §6.4](worker-host-api-v9.md) line 230: "`git push` to the internal Git service and to a connected GitHub or GitLab repository needs no token in the VM." [design §6.5](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 566: the proxy "recovers the real host from the path, re-originates over HTTPS and injects the `git_https` credential". [plan P4.12](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 887 item 3: "`/git/<host>/…` paths re-originated to the real host with the `git_https` credential". [plan P1.5](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 345: "the only provider interactions are (a) inbound mirroring of protected branches and (b) explicit server-side publish". | N10: rewrite worker-host-api line 230 so external hosts are clone/fetch only and publication happens only on the control plane; extend the grant to `{ kind: git_https, target_host, repositories, operations, source }` with `receive_pack` valid only for `source: workspace_token`; canonical scheme/host/port/path-prefix matching and redirect handling at the proxy; the v8 review line 56 regression in P4.12. |
| S5 (P1): transfer's interim guard misses queued legacy work | Partially resolved in the contract with an owning PR (P4.5 step 8): the guard, its error code, the recheck under admission and the removal condition are specified, but the guard still keys on active execution, and the frozen provider-account resolver is called out only in plan prose, not in P4.5's steps or tests. | [plan P4.5](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 678: "refuse transfer with a clear `WORKSPACE_LEGACY_EXECUTION_ACTIVE` blocker while any such execution is active ... A safely stopped/drained workspace may transfer. Test the actual retained task/sticky state owners, races with their launch, and recheck under admission before owner commit." [plan P4.5](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 666: "Apply current-owner authorization to retained legacy task/job/chat routes now, before their dispatcher migration". [design §13](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 1007: "P4.5 refuses transfer for a workspace with retained active legacy execution until that work is drained or migrated". [plan §3.5](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 132: "`ProviderAccountService::resolve_frozen_for_dispatch` and its `config_service.rs` caller currently resolve a saved account id; v9 must not treat a prior owner's saved id as current authority." [plan P4.7](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 740 and [plan P4.8](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 764: the blocker is removed only after every producer is under the workspace stop protocol. | N59: widen the guard to every nonterminal legacy state (`pending|claimed|running|paused|blocked|recovering`, sticky runtimes, unacknowledged claims) taken under the lifecycle lock and rechecked before the owner CAS; add the transfer barrier and frozen-owner predicate to the retained claim UPDATE in task_service.rs; add the v8 review line 66 regression (queued job, no capacity, transfer, then capacity returns) to P4.5's tests. |
| S6 (P2): removing read access does not revoke established streams | Mentioned without a mechanism. Closing owner live streams on ownership or access change is a repeated requirement, and P5.1 and P2.1 name an "authorization generation" as a binding key, but no document defines where the generation lives, the close-after-commit ordering, the bounded delay, or coverage of reader durable subscriptions (chat/timeline, task events, branch/flush/projection hints), which are the streams the v8 finding was about. Reader live file streams no longer exist in v9, which narrows the exposure. | [design §9.1](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 858: "Access is checked per request and stream admission. ... stop, transfer and revocation close streams and invalidate that client's live caches before any read-only rendering. Non-owners receive ref/projection invalidation hints". [design §6.6](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 595: "revoke streams and cached live content on ownership/access changes". [design §6.8](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 631: "existing connections close at stop/transfer". [design §14](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 1041: "close streams on revocation". [workspace-sharing](workspace-sharing-v9.md) line 35: "Ownership/access changes close live streams, invalidate live responses and clear source-dependent client/server caches". [workspace-vm-api §2](workspace-vm-api-v9.md) line 54 and [worker-host-api §3.1](worker-host-api-v9.md) line 89: the same statement for VM streams. [plan P5.1](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 957: "Tickets/streams bind current owner, authorization generation and VM incarnation; recheck opening/reattachment and close on transfer, demotion, revocation or stop." [plan P2.1](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 395: "Demotion, transfer, access revocation and incarnation changes close live watchers/streams". [plan P8.8](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 1219: "revoked read/preview tickets ... Audit cache/subscription invalidation after demotion/transfer". | N49: an `access_generation` per (user, workspace) incremented in the membership/visibility/owner transaction, admission of every browser subscription (owner live and reader durable) with that generation, hub closure of older subscriptions after commit, refusal of reconnects and tickets carrying the old generation, and a stated bound; the v8 review line 76 regression at the hub boundary. |
| passt/metadata validation: guest access to host infrastructure through passt | Silent on a host-enforced guest egress policy. The only related v9 text is the dispatcher-enforced module "network egress policy" capability (guest-side, so it does not constrain traffic that bypasses the dispatcher) and the open passt question, which concerns telling VMs apart at the proxy, not what else the guest can reach. | [worker-host-api §6](worker-host-api-v9.md) line 175: the guest "reaches host loopback through the passt door `169.254.1.2`"; line 177: "every VM's passt maps the door to the same host `127.0.0.1`" and the secret "grants nothing but access to that VM's own proxy". [worker-host-api §13](worker-host-api-v9.md) line 363: the open question is whether a per-VM host address would make "the proxy secret defense in depth only". [design §6.7](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 605 and [workspace-vm-api §3](workspace-vm-api-v9.md) line 68: a module manifest requests a "network egress policy" that the dispatcher intersects with the grant. [plan P4.12](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 885 item 1 and [plan P4.4b](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) lines 637 to 660: passt door, per-VM listener and boot, no network rules. Searched with no result: design §6.3, §6.5, §6.10, §14, §16, §17; worker-host-api §4, §5, §6, §7.1, §10, §11, §12; plan P4.4b, P4.4a, P4.12, P8.8 and §7.1; a grep over all seven documents for egress, metadata server, nftables, no-map-host-loopback, 169.254, firewall and network rule. | N8: a host-installed, fail-closed ruleset before boot (nftables keyed on the passt uid or cgroup, or passt launched with `--no-map-host-loopback` plus explicit forwards) allowing only the per-VM proxy port on the door, DNS through passt and an org allowlist, and denying 169.254.0.0/16 and other host listeners; the v8 review line 84 validation (direct IPs, DNS aliases, redirects, IPv6, proxied and direct traffic) on the SaaS and self-hosted paths. N9: an owning PR (P4.4b) and test before VM admission is enabled. |

The net change from v8 to v9 is a stricter access model on an unchanged isolation model. V9 shrinks the set of principals that can touch a workspace (one owner writes and executes, readers browse durable Git and metadata only, preview, terminal and live streams are owner-only, laptop pushes need a running admission-open workspace, each incarnation is bound to one owner's provider account, provider credentials stay on the host), and that removes two v8 exposure paths outright: the reader-viewed owner preview that made S2 a cross-user trust crossing, and the reader live-file streams that S6 covered. It also gives S5 its first specified mechanism and owning PR. It does not add any mechanism for object-level Git confidentiality (S1), browser-origin isolation (S2), separation of the trusted runtime from repository processes inside the guest (S3), a repository- and operation-bound external grant (S4), generation-based stream revocation (S6) or host-enforced guest egress (passt); the design says so itself at [design §0](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 28 and [design §6.8](lean-backend-design-v9.md) line 631. Because Git is now the only read path for every non-owner, v9 depends more on the S1 boundary than v8 did while leaving it unchanged. No v8 finding is closed by v9 text; S5 is partially resolved with an owning PR, S4 is deferred in the design while the companion contradicts it, S1, S2, S3 and S6 are acknowledged without mechanisms, and the passt item is silent.

## Verification and test plan for AI-agent implementation

A test counts only if it fails without the behavior and drives the authoritative production boundary (a real route, socket, git ref or process), not a helper mirror ([plan §7.1](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 1366). Every PR description must separate five claims: implemented (the diff exists), locally validated (the ladder of [plan §2 item 8](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 28 ran on this head), exact-head CI validated (`g5-ci-rust` green on the candidate, `g5-ci-e2e` classified stage by stage), deployed (a QA step that has no owner today, N63) and observed (a person or receipt saw the loop on QA). Review completion and CI support the first three and prove nothing about the last two.

### Layered strategy

The five contexts proposed overlapping stacks; merged, they form one ladder. Owning PRs are those whose prompts add tests at that layer.

| Layer | Purpose | Harness (exists / new) | What it proves | Owning PRs |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| L0 Static and absence guards | Removed surfaces stay gone; single writers stay single | exists: `route_auth_static.rs`, `check-execution-lifecycle-writers.py` ([cloudbuild-ci-rust.yaml](../infra/ci/cloudbuild-ci-rust.yaml) line 909); new: router-inventory snapshot, action-registry test, protocol fixture corpus | no PR reintroduces Commit/Flush-now/search/CRDT, a second `tasks` writer or a branch-creating fallback; a renamed wire field breaks a fixture | P2.2, P2.4, P4.3a, P4.4a, P4.5, P4.6, P4.14, P8.9 |

### Per-PR definition of done

Run on the exact head; paste each command and outcome under Tests and Validation run ([plan §2.1](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 35).

1. `SQLX_OFFLINE=true cargo check --workspace --all-targets --offline`, `just fmt-check`, `just lint` ([justfile](../justfile) lines 819, 813) and `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings` all exit 0.
2. `SQLX_OFFLINE=true cargo test -p <crate> --lib` per touched crate passes and lists every acceptance test the prompt names.
3. `just dev-up` then `just test-integration` (line 668) when routes, services or the dispatcher changed: 0 failures.
4. `npm --prefix frontend run build && npm --prefix frontend test` when `frontend/` changed, plus `just ci-docker-7 playwright_filter='<spec>'` with the per-test JSON receipt attached.
5. `just docker-ci` for worker, host, isolation, dispatcher or CI changes, stating whether it ran with KVM/HVF or process only.
6. Migration checks of [plan §2 item 6](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 26 (fresh, upgrade on real rows, no-op rerun), checksum appended, `just db-prepare` (line 3050) regenerated.
7. Every deleted or moved test in `git diff origin/main --stat` is classified: deleted protocol, covered at HEAD (naming the cover) or rehomed (naming the path).
8. Mutation run: rsync to a scratch copy, disable the production change behind the tests the context names (lease arming, attempt fence, early publish, stdin lifetime, generation check), paste the failing assertion, confirm green on the real tree.
9. Fault-injection matrix: the crash and race points the PR owns (C1 to C12, T1 to T3, M1 to M7, F6, delivery_unknown, harvest failure) each have one named test; an untested point goes under Blocked (N13, N33, N98, N102).
10. `g5-ci-rust` green on the exact head; each red `g5-ci-e2e` stage named and classified against a main-equivalent build.
11. `grep -rn "LEAN-TODO(P<id>)" backend frontend` returns nothing unresolved ([plan §2 item 5](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 25).
12. Status row filled with branch, PR link and one Notes line; Depends on untouched ([plan §2 item 10](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 30).
13. Process/manual: per clause of [plan §7.1](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 1347, state which of implemented, locally validated, exact-head CI validated, deployed and observed holds.

### Test-strategy artifact

[Plan §2 item 7](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 27 requires the strategy to be agreed before the first runtime PR but names no owner, file or gate (N95). It should be `docs/lean-test-strategy-v9.md`, landed by a docs-only PR before P4.2a opens and edited by each owning PR in the same diff:

1. Purpose and the five evidence levels; a skipped retained test is not a pass.
2. The layer table with harness status and file paths.
3. Harness inventory (`ScriptedHostClient` (N70, the one host-protocol peer), `FakeVmm`, `RepoVectorFixture`, `RuntimeTripwire`, fault hooks, `TestClock`, fixture corpora) with owning PR and landing order (N70, N68).
4. Fault-injection register: every crash, race, stale-event and partial-recovery point with layer, PR and test name; open rows for N2, N13, N33, N40.
5. Security register: each open v8 finding mapped to an MVP PR and test (N9, N3, N46).
6. CI lane map with gate status, flip dates and the flake rerun policy (N61).
7. Removed-test ledger, appended per PR.
8. QA observation protocol: deployer, loops walked, receipt location (N63, N44).
9. Change log, one line per editing PR.

#### Boundary tests

Receive-pack ([design §5.3](lean-backend-design-v9.md) lines 280–293; extends [api_git.rs](../backend/g5-api/tests/api_git.rs) lines 788, 955, 1161; owner P4.3a unless noted):

1. `receive_pack_matrix_differential`: per L1 cell, mint the JWT or incarnation token, build the pkt-line with real OIDs, `POST /git/:repo/git-receive-pack`; assert status, error code, `git rev-parse <ref>` unchanged on deny.
2. `flush_ref_create_only_from_current_incarnation`: create succeeds for `vm_id == row.vm_id` in `running` and `stopping`; update/delete denied for all network identities; stale `vm_id` refused before advertisement (line 291); server identity deletes on prune.
3. `forged_fork_receipt_denied`: user and incarnation create/update/delete of `refs/g5/forks/<uuid>` denied (line 287). P4.5.
4. `workspace_branch_recreate_denied_even_with_matching_mapping`: delete the mapped branch with plain git; zero-old create from laptop and incarnation denied; restore reports integrity error ([plan P4.3a](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 589).
5. `laptop_push_requires_running_vm_and_open_admission`: `stopped` gives `WORKSPACE_NOT_RUNNING` with zero `ScriptedHostClient` commands and tripwire frames; `stopping` gives `WORKSPACE_STOPPING`; push admitted during a transfer held by `DeferredCommitGate` on `owner_user_id`: exactly one wins (line 293). P4.5.
6. `only_fork_service_creates_branches_static`: source-text test like [git.rs](../backend/g5-api-routes/src/routes/git.rs) line 1645: `mint_canonical_branch_name`/`generate_branch_name` ([workspace_service.rs](../backend/g5-api-core/src/services/workspace_service.rs) line 14841) gone; only `workspace_fork_service.rs` calls the create-if-absent helper. P4.5.

Flush and restore ([design §5.4](lean-backend-design-v9.md) lines 320–329, §5.5 lines 351–355; `backend/g5d-dispatch/tests/flush_restore.rs` against a `file://` origin and a `TestApp` smart-HTTP origin; each scenario ends with the content-walk oracle: `git hash-object` of every tracked or untracked-not-ignored path equals `git ls-tree -r S`; owner P4.3a):

7. `snapshot_recipe_matches_design_text`: execute lines 320–324 literally; tree OID equals the implementation's.
8. `tracked_ignored_file_dirty_edit_captured`: tracked file later ignored; edit in `S`; untracked file under the same rule absent (line 327, §15 line 1066).
9. `racy_same_second_same_size_edit`: `git add` v1, `utimes` index mtime <= file mtime, same-size v2 preserving mtime; `S` has v2; index newer than a backdated file pinned as undetectable.
10. `symlinks_modes_renames_deletes`: file/dir/dangling symlinks, symlink replaced by file, `chmod +x`, dirty rename and delete; restore removes HEAD-tracked paths absent from `S` and recreates links (line 352).
11. `warm_root_same_head_same_paths_different_bytes`: equal HEAD, dirty paths and sizes, different bytes; attach writes receipt bytes and removes untracked non-ignored extras (line 355).
12. `committed_unpushed_clean_tree`: `S` skipped, `snapshot_oid == local_head_oid`; after restore `git rev-list origin/<branch>..HEAD` is N and `git ls-remote` shows `origin/<branch>` unchanged (line 351).
13. `force_with_lease_after_flush_still_protects`: `--force-with-lease=<branch>:<stale>` discarding an unseen commit still refused after a flush.
14. `provider_session_files_never_captured`: `~/.claude`, Codex home, `.g5/` absent from `S`; native `git add -A` does not add them (P4.2a line 534).
15. `unborn_lfs_gitlink_in_progress_pinned`: unborn member, `*.bin filter=lfs`, gitlink, conflicted rebase, detached HEAD, stash; each yields a named error or recorded loss (gaps 1–3).
16. `two_repo_second_push_fails`: ST4 hook on repo2; receipt incomplete, previous receipt kept, retry succeeds, orphan ref pruned after 24 h (§15 line 1065).
17. `flush_reason_rejected`: `explicit`, unknown, and `fork` before P4.3b refused; no ref write, no temp index left.
18. `flush_restore_identity_property`: the proptest below.

Receipts and projections (§5.4 lines 329, 341; §9.4 line 876; extends [projection_convergence.rs](../backend/g5-api/tests/projection_convergence.rs); P4.3a unless noted):

19. `receipt_complete_verifies_every_ref`: ST5; `complete_at` NULL, `last_flush_id` unchanged.
20. `prune_keeps_two_newest_and_pending_fork_sources`: K1; no dangling `source_flush_id`.
21. `projection_convergence_covers_flush_refs`: P1; snapshot ref projected with the bus disabled; prune removes its `projection_state` row; `head_sha_cache` repaired from git.
22. `docs_projection_for_selected_receipt_oid_or_explicit_unavailable`: reader selects an older receipt; docs route returns that OID's projection or `PROJECTION_UNAVAILABLE`, never live. P2.1.

Fork ([design §5.8](lean-backend-design-v9.md) lines 398–403; [plan P4.5](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) lines 683–684, 694; `RepoVectorFixture`; P4.5 unless noted):

23. `fork_two_repos_plus_docs_exact_name`: `feature/x-1` byte-identical in all three mirrors at pinned OIDs; defaults untouched; child `stopped`, `owner_resume_required`, owner is caller (§15 line 1063).
24. `fork_collision_only_in_docs_repo_creates_nothing`: 409 `WORKSPACE_NAME_CONFLICT` naming docs; `git for-each-ref` unchanged everywhere; reservations released.
25. `fork_prefix_and_identical_oid_conflict`: `feature` blocks `feature/x` and vice versa; a branch already at the source OID still conflicts (line 399).
26. `fork_provider_only_collision_and_unavailable`: provider remote holds the name: conflict; `chmod 000` on it: retryable error, no refs, same operation retried (C8).
27. `fork_concurrent_overlapping_sets_reverse_order`: A(repo1,repo2) and B(repo2,repo1) under `tokio::join`; one completes, one 409, no deadlock in 10 s; never two `fork_id`s per (repo, name) (line 400).
28. `fork_crash_matrix`: C1–C9, each recovered in `spawn_attached`; pending child not runnable (`POST resume` refused); refs equal manifest; same key returns same child; different payload refused.
29. `reader_fork_of_running_source_uses_receipt_not_live`: receipt R (v1), branch advanced to v2, VM `running` with tripwire; child at `R.local_head_oid`, content v1 and differs from live, zero frames, source `workspace_vms` row unchanged ([plan P4.3b](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 603). P4.3b.
30. `owner_live_fork_busy_then_interrupt`: turn running: `WORKSPACE_BUSY{exec_ids}`; `interrupt=true`: consistent two-repo capture, source stays running, admission reopens (line 604). P4.3b.
31. `fork_flush_quiescence`: Q1; barrier reopens after completion, refusal and failure. P4.3b.
32. `stop_during_fork_flush_serializes`: the second waits or is refused; `last_flush_id` is the newest completed receipt regardless of `flush_id` order (§5.4 line 331). P4.3b.

Stop, transfer, Save and reads (§5.8 lines 426–434, §4.3 line 176, §9.1 lines 851–858; `ScriptedHostClient` plus dispatcher child; P4.5 unless noted):

33. `stop_sequence_happy_path_ordering`: observed order close, interrupt, `flush(shutdown)`, harvest, `vm.stop graceful`, `exited`, release; `stopping` with one `vm_id` throughout; a flush-ref create by that `vm_id` succeeds mid-sequence.
34. `in_flight_save_admitted_before_close_lands_in_receipt`: ST2; a Save after close gets `WORKSPACE_STOPPING` and is absent.
35. `user_stop_flush_fails_vm_stays_up`: ST4 with reason `user`; a Save succeeds afterwards; `last_error` set; no `vm.stop` in the `ScriptedHostClient` log.
36. `drain_flush_fails_proceeds_at_deadline`: ST4 with reason `drain`; next resume restores the previous receipt.
37. `idle_stop_flush_fails_cancels_timer` and `reads_do_not_extend_activity`: durable reads leave `last_activity_at` unchanged; owner live `fs.read` updates it (line 858).
38. `host_exited_event_lost_then_lifecycle_restart`: ST7 recovered by `spawn_attached`.
39. `resume_during_stopping_waits`: `POST resume` while stopping returns 202; one incarnation with epoch+1 after release; never two `vm.start` per workspace (ST9).
40. `stale_incarnation_fenced_everywhere_after_release`: ST10 across receive-pack, upload-pack, harvest, flush complete, facade reply, event routes (P4.5 line 672).
41. `transfer_running_workspace`: admission closes (Save `WORKSPACE_STOPPING`, laptop push refused), flush, exit, release, then owner CAS plus audit in one transaction held by `DeferredCommitGate`; old-owner facade handle refused (§7.1 line 1356).
42. `transfer_crash_after_release_before_owner_commit`: T1.
43. `transfer_stopped_workspace_no_vm_work`: owner to member and admin to self; zero `ScriptedHostClient` commands; identical repeat returns the same operation; T3 aborts (line 679).
44. `sibling_mutations_share_boundary`: table-driven over files PUT, upload, delete, rename, docs, ontology, question-doc: stopped gives `WORKSPACE_NOT_RUNNING` with zero `vm.start`; stale hash 409 with current content; stale `vm_id` conflict; stop between authorization and mutation leaves the write out of the receipt; queued Save replayed under a new incarnation refused ([plan P2.1](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 396, §15 line 1074). P2.1.
45. `read_source_matrix_all_cells`: durable cells via `TestApp`, live cells via `ScriptedHostClient`; `source`, per-repo OID, `captured_at` per lines 851–854; incomplete receipt ignored; R2 stream closed with no later live payload (§7.1 line 1352). P2.1.
46. `newer_branch_push_than_receipt_is_labeled`: default view flush R with `committed_newer: true`; R1 returns one OID (line 856). P2.1.
47. KVM-gated `workspace-vm-lifecycle` group in `backend/g5d-host` tests: edit, stop, resume restores dirty and unpushed work; warm reflink root equals cold bytes; test 9 on virtiofs; `vm.stop graceful` as SIGTERM to the VMM process group then SIGKILL at `grace_secs` (N101); superseded VM killed on reconnect and its flush push refused 403; `scriptedhost_vs_realhost_transcript_contract` diffs the `ScriptedHostClient` script transcript against the real host modulo timestamps. P4.3a, P4.5.
48. Playwright ([plan P4.10](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 841; P2.2 line 423): `explicit-save.spec.ts` (Ctrl+S while stopped issues zero writes via `page.route`), `workspace-vm.spec.ts`, `changes-and-push.spec.ts` (HEAD unchanged after Push; removed Commit routes 404), `association-mismatch.spec.ts`, `fork-dialog.spec.ts` (submitted name equals branch names via API), `reader-and-owner-views.spec.ts` (zero `/vm`, `/ports`, `/watch` requests). P2.2, P4.10.
49. Value loops in [tests/e2e](../tests/e2e/value_loop_matrix.tsv): `value_loop_edit_stop_resume`, `value_loop_reader_fork_while_owner_busy`, `value_loop_transfer`; rows VL-01, VL-02, VL-10, VL-11 retargeted from CRDT/worker-token semantics or retired with reasons. P4.5, P2.4.

#### Fault-injection points

| Point | Design ref | Mechanism to inject | Expected observation |
|---|---|---|---|
| C1 fork: crash after reservations, before manifest pinned | §5.8 lines 400, 403 | failpoint `fork.after_reserve`; `spawn_attached` | no refs; reservations released only after resolution; same key, same operation |
| C2 fork: repo1 ref transaction done, ack lost | §5.8 line 401 | failpoint `fork.after_ref_txn[repo1]` | recovery recognizes `refs/g5/forks/<fork_id>` at the planned OID, not a collision |
| C3 fork: foreign ref appears in repo2 after preflight | §5.8 lines 399, 403 | `git update-ref` on the mirror during a pause | 409 after cleanup; repo1 refs deleted by expected-OID CAS; foreign ref untouched |
| C4 fork: repo2 ref write refused | §5.8 line 403; P4.5 line 694 | `obstruct_branch_reflog` or `update` hook | pending, reservations held; retry keeps the pinned source vector |
| C5 fork: operation-created ref advanced before rollback | §5.8 line 403 | advance repo1's new branch during the C3 pause | rollback refuses that delete; child unavailable; nothing else deleted |
| C6 fork: publish COMMIT lost after refs verified | §5.8 line 402 | `DeferredCommitGate` on `workspaces` | idempotent republish; one child row; reservations released once |
| C7 fork: crash between publish and release or carry | §5.8 line 402 | failpoints `fork.after_publish`, `fork.after_release` | carry finishes once; a second fork conflicts on the real ref |
| C8 fork: provider verification hangs | §5.8 line 398 | provider remote replaced by a FIFO | retryable error, no refs, not a free name |
| C9 fork racing transfer, deletion, member change, revocation | §5.8 line 401; P4.5 line 682 | `tokio::join` plus pause per ordering | frozen set completes or explicit failure; never mixed members |
| ST1 reconciler tick after decide, before close | §5.8 line 426 | failpoint `stop.after_decide`; call reconciler | no loss, `vm_id` unchanged |
| ST2 admitted write completes after close | §5.8 line 427; §5.4 line 333 | dispatcher failpoint `fs.write.after_admit` | close returns after the write; write in receipt |
| ST3 module ignores SIGINT | §5.8 line 428 | fake-provider `ignore-sigint` | SIGKILL at 15 s; attempt `interrupted`; flush proceeds |
| ST4 flush push of repo2 refused | §5.4 lines 337–339 | `update` hook exit 1 | user/transfer/idle abort with VM up; deadline reasons release with `unflushed_loss_at` |
| ST5 receipt verification fails | §5.4 line 329 | delete flush ref during `flush.before_complete` pause | complete refused; cold resume uses previous receipt |
| ST6 harvest fails or ack lost | §5.8 line 430 | 500 once; tunnel drop after reply | no release before harvest durable; idempotent by (`vm_id`, epoch) |
| ST7 `exited` and `stopped(exit)` lost | §5.8 line 431 | `ScriptedHostClient` drops tunnel after `vm.stop` | stays `stopping`; re-send with same key; never `stale` |
| ST8 crash after flush; after exit | §5.8 lines 431–432 | failpoints `stop.after_flush`, `stop.after_exit` | one release; `last_flush_id` equals the receipt |
| ST9 resume or restart during stopping | §5.8 line 431 | concurrent POST while `ScriptedHostClient` delays exit | waits; no `workspace_busy`; epoch+1 once |
| ST10 old incarnation acts after release | §5.3 line 291; §6.4 | keep released token and child alive | every boundary refuses; reconnect kills without flush |
| T1 transfer crash after release, before owner CAS | §5.8 line 434 | `tokio::select!` cancel at `stopped` | one audit row; owner set once; no VM |
| T2 old-owner push during transfer commit | §5.3 line 293 | `DeferredCommitGate` on the final transaction | push lands only if admitted before the barrier |
| T3 target leaves org; competing transfers | §5.8 line 434; P4.5 line 679 | membership delete under pause; two POSTs | abort with error; one winner |
| R1 branch advances between tree and content | §9.1 line 856 | plain push during a paused second request | same OID or a labeled new vector |
| R2 owner demoted with open `fs.watch` | §9.1 line 858; P2.1 line 395 | transfer while a WS client subscribes, then agent writes | stream closed; next fetch labeled flush |
| P1 ref written, no event | §9.4 line 876 | plain-git write; receipt completed with bus disabled | converged in one pass; prune removes rows |
| Q1 Save admitted before a fork barrier closes | §5.4 line 333 | `fs.write.after_admit` plus `fork` flush | wholly in both repos or in neither |
| K1 prune vs pending fork | §5.4 line 341; §5.8 line 401 | three flushes while a manifest pins N-3 | referenced refs kept or explicit fork failure |

#### Model-based and property tests

- Receive-pack table (§5.3 lines 280–289) as `fn decide_ref_update(identity, ref_name, old, new, ws_row, vm_row, reservations) -> Decision`; enumerate identity (owner, non-owner, former owner, current/stale/draining incarnation, server merge/fork/bootstrap) x nine namespaces x {create, ff, non-ff, delete} x ref state; any unlisted cell fails; proptest names with `git check-ref-format` as oracle, conflict iff equal or `/`-prefix related; differential subset through test 1. P4.3a.
- Fork state machine (§5.8 lines 398–403): proptest sequences of crash at step k, retry same/different payload, overlapping fork, foreign ref, provider down, access revoked; invariants: runnable implies all refs at pinned OIDs, receipt verified, manifest complete; ref exists iff manifest says created; rollback CAS only on planned OIDs; replayed against `TestApp` with failpoints. P4.5.
- Flush then restore identity (§5.4–§5.5): random sequences of create, modify, same-size modify, delete, rename, chmod, symlink, ignore rule, tracked-then-ignored, unicode names on two repos; after cold and warm restore the content-walk oracle is byte-identical, HEAD equal, changes unstaged, `git ls-files -s` unchanged, `origin/<branch>` unchanged. P4.3a.
- Read-source matrix (§9.1 lines 851–854): role x VM status x attachment x receipt presence x base/working; `Live` only for owner and running and attached; each cell checked in test 45. P2.1.
- Lifecycle and transfer machine (§5.8 lines 426–434): proptest over stop(reason), flush ok/fail, deadline, exited, event lost, resume, transfer, reconciler tick, restart; `stopped` implies `last_flush_id` xor `unflushed_loss_at`; `vm_id` cleared only at release; never two current incarnations; user/transfer/idle never release after a failed flush. P4.5.
- Admission barrier vs capture (§5.4 line 333): seeded interleavings of Save/delete/rename against a fork flush; each mutation wholly in or out. P4.3b.
- Reservation lock ordering (§5.8 line 400): overlapping sets and orderings, reverse order always included; bounded wait, one winner per (repo, name). P4.5.
- Receipt retention (§5.4 line 341): prune over complete/incomplete flushes, pending manifests, deletions; the two newest complete receipts and every pending-fork source survive. P4.3a.

#### Gaps in design §15 and the plan

| Gap | Where | Recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| Unborn branches: `commit-tree -p HEAD` has no parent, `checkout -B` has no OID, fork of an unborn default has no rule | §5.4 line 324, §5.5 line 351; §15, P4.3a, P4.5 silent | onboarding bootstraps a root commit; fork and flush refuse unborn refs with a named error; test 15 |
| LFS, filters, gitlinks, embedded repos: flush stores pointers/gitlinks, restore is lossy or fails | §5.4 line 327, §5.5 line 352 | MVP position: unsupported, flush fails `UNSUPPORTED_REPOSITORY_FEATURE`, drain records loss; test 15 |
| In-progress rebase/merge, detached HEAD, stash: receipt holds only `local_head_oid`; restore drops rebase state and pre-rebase tip | §5.4 recipe, §5.5 line 351, §5.8 line 428 | add `head_symbolic`, `detached`, `in_progress_operation` to the receipt; refuse user stop/transfer mid-rebase or surface the loss; test 15 |
| "Same-second same-size edit" under-specified; virtiofs stat semantics unverified | §15 line 1066; P4.3a line 583 | define as test 9 with the oracle; repeat in test 47; record `core.checkStat`/`core.trustctime` in §17.1 |
| Restore of HEAD-tracked paths absent from the snapshot, modes, symlink-to-file replacement unspecified | §5.5 line 352; P4.3a line 580 | state removal and mode/type application; test 10 and the identity property |
| "Zero VM calls" has no observable | §15 line 1080; P2.1 lines 396–397; P4.3b line 603; §7.1 line 1352 | require `RuntimeCallLog`, `RuntimeTripwire`, `ScriptedHostClient` log and row invariants in every reader test |
| Fork crash coverage stops at "after each member ref transaction" | §15 line 1063; P4.5 line 684 | add C5–C9 and `spawn_attached` recovery to P4.5; fork model with differential replay |
| Prune can delete a pending fork's or a reader-selected receipt | §5.4 line 341; §5.8 line 401 | prune skips refs in pending manifests or fork fails explicitly; K1, retention model |
| No rule for a selected receipt OID without a projection row | §9.1 lines 852–856, §9.4 line 876; P2.1 line 394 | specify `PROJECTION_UNAVAILABLE` or on-demand projection; test 22 |
| Ladder and [run-integration.sh](../scripts/run-integration.sh) lines 36–37 omit the dispatcher crate; no KVM lifecycle step; [cloudbuild-ci-rust.yaml](../infra/ci/cloudbuild-ci-rust.yaml) lines 1362–1368 silently degrade without KVM | plan §2 items 8, 11 (lines 28, 31) | add `cargo test -p g5d-dispatch --test '*'` in P4.2a/P4.3a; authorize P4.3a/P4.5 to add a fail-closed `workspace-vm-lifecycle` step |
| P2.4 deletes `api_drafts.rs`, sole home of `LandingCommitGate` and `obstruct_branch_reflog` | P2.4 line 438; P4.5 line 684 | P4.5 rehomes them plus the hook helper into `tests/helpers`; P2.4 lists them as rehomed |
| Playwright acceptance relies on `g5-ci-e2e`, which fails constantly and skips stages | P2.2 line 423; P4.10 line 849 | require per-test `G5_TEST_END` JSON from `just ci-docker 7 false "" "<spec>"` in the PR; isolated Playwright project with a private seed |
| Reader fork rule lacks the positive assertion (child equals receipt, differs from live) | §15 line 1080; P4.3b line 603 | two-sided assertion plus zero-frame observables (test 29) |
| Upload-pack advertisement of `refs/g5/*` to readers unspecified; mixed atomic pushes implicit | §5.3 line 297, lines 280–289 | advertise `refs/heads/*` and `refs/g5/flush/<own>/*` only; exhaustive table plus test 1 |
| Flush serialization and UUIDv7 ordering asserted, not tested | §5.4 line 331 | test 32 in P4.3b |
| No property or model testing dependency | plan §2 item 7 line 27; `Cargo.lock` | `proptest` workspace dev-dependency in P4.3a; the eight model tests are retained tests |

### Worker and Host

Scope: [design §6.2–§6.5](lean-backend-design-v9.md) lines 481–574, §6.10 lines 639–649, and the normative [worker-host-api §2–§8](worker-host-api-v9.md) lines 22–296. Gate on g5-ci-rust only ([cloudbuild-ci-rust.yaml](../infra/ci/cloudbuild-ci-rust.yaml) `rust-test` line 1053, `rust-integration` line 1117, `rust-integration-g5d` line 1195, `smoke-libkrun-quick` line 1666); g5-ci-e2e has constant failing stages and never gates this context. Citations below: "D" is the design, "N" is the note, "P" is the plan, all by line.

#### Boundary tests

Files: A `backend/g5d-host/tests/ctl_conformance.rs` (real `g5d host`, `FakeVmm`, `ScriptedControlPlane`); B `backend/g5-api/tests/api_host_ctl.rs` (real g5-api, Postgres, `ScriptedHostClient`); C `backend/g5d-host/tests/host_dispatcher_process.rs` (real host, real `g5d dispatch` process, `FakeProviderUpstream`); D `backend/g5-api/tests/api_worker_hosts.rs`, `api_rollouts.rs`; E `backend/g5d/tests/workspace_vm_boot.rs` (`--features microvm-e2e`, [Cargo.toml](../backend/g5d/Cargo.toml) line 19); F `tests/e2e/src/scenarios/host_partition_reconnect.rs`, `host_rollout.rs`. A, C: `cargo test -p g5d-host --test <file>` in nextest group `host-protocol = { max-threads = 1 }` ([nextest.toml](../.config/nextest.toml) line 17). B, D: `just test-integration`. E: the KVM lanes above. F: local KVM or QA.

Versioning and envelope:
1. A: unsupported `protocol_version` in `host.hello`; assert `host.reject`, `vm.start` refused by name, two `FakeVmm` VMs and timers kept, backoff reconnect, each VM `lease_expired_killed` exactly at TTL (D 496; N 325); P4.4a.
2. A: `vm.start` with `deadline = now - 1 s` and `= now`; assert `expired`, no spawn, no timer, no slot (D 494); P4.4a.
3. A: 17 blocked `vm.status`; assert the 17th `busy`; same-key retry after capacity returns the original result (D 510); P4.4a.
4. A: 2 MiB `VmSpec`; assert `spec_rejected(too_large)` with the request id, no silent drop (N 289; G7); P4.4a.
5. B: previous corpus version decodes through the real tunnel route; two versions back gets `host.reject` (P 1194); P8.7.

Retained results and journal:
6. A: socket closed on first `vm.start`; assert caller `timeout`, inventory lists the VM, same-key retry at `t < 600 s` returns `started`, at `t > 600 s` `not_found` (D 498; N 323); P4.4a.
7. A: `FakeVmm` exits with the tunnel down; assert `vm.event exited` is the first frame after `host.accept` (D 508); P4.4a.
8. A: `vm.event.ack` cumulative; a lower out-of-order ack never resurrects events; P4.4a.
9. A: assert_cmd child `g5d host` `kill -9` between `exited` and ack, restart on the same state dir; assert replay with the same `seq`, no gap (G4); P4.4a.
10. B: same `(vm_id, seq)` sent twice; assert one row, two acks; P4.4a.

Credential and heartbeat:
11. A+B: `host.credential.renew` refused while `draining`, `terminated`, after identity change; old credential holds the tunnel to expiry; the 24 h JWT path returns 401 (D 485; P 627); P4.4a.
12. B: heartbeat ack lists `starting|running|stopping`, omits `failed|stopped`; `lease_expires_at` unchanged after 10 acks (D 508); P4.4a.
13. A: ack computed before an accepted `vm.start(C)` arrives after it; assert C's first stream open and proxy request pass (G3); P4.4b.

Lease and fence:
14. C: dispatcher `lease.renew` on `rpc` forwarded on `ctl`; token installed only when its `vm_id` claim matches (D 553); P4.4b.
15. C, `TestClock`: reply held `d` in `{0, ttl/2, ttl, ttl+1}` then partition; assert host deadline before grant, proxy `403 stale_incarnation` before kill, `lease_expired_killed` on reconnect, replacement only after grant (D 554, 557, 1025); P4.4b.
16. A: reply `seq n` after `n+1`; assert timer armed from `n+1` departure (D 554); P4.4b.
17. C: `stale` renewal; assert one `stale_killed`, process dead (N 165); P4.4b.
18. A: current set holds only B; send `vm.start(A)`, `vm.grants.update(A)`, open `rpc|pty|http` for A, `vm.stop(A)`; assert `stale` x3 and `stopped(exit)`; a `stopping` incarnation passes everywhere (D 494, 555); P4.4b.
19. A: inventory holds a `vm_id` absent from `current_incarnations`; assert kill without stop sequence, `stale_killed` (D 556); P4.4b.
20. A, `TestClock`: `vm.stop` twice at `t < 600 s`, once after; assert `stopped(exit)` x2, then `not_found` (N 98; G9); P4.4b.

Spec and slots:
21. A: wrong `org_id` plus forbidden env key; assert `org_mismatch`; token mismatch plus unresolved grant; assert `boot_failed(grant_unresolved)`, no spawn, slot freed (D 540); P4.4b.
22. A: `vm.start(W, A)` then `(W, B)`; assert `slot_unavailable(workspace_busy)`; three held boots; third `slot_unavailable(boot_capacity)` (D 502); P4.4b.
23. A: `workspaces_root` on a fixed-size tmpfs; assert oldest root evicted, heartbeat `retained_roots` shrinks, then `slot_unavailable(disk)` (D 546); P4.4b.
24. C: `vm.stop graceful`; assert SIGTERM to the VMM process group, SIGKILL at `grace_secs` (N101; no guest-side signal is asserted), slot freed after reap, proxy listener closed (D 503); P4.4b.

Proxies (C; steering by `repoint_env_to_proxy`, [llm_proxy.rs](../backend/g5d-worker/src/llm_proxy.rs) line 387):
25. fake-claude probe via `ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:<port>/s/<session>`; assert upstream saw the owner credential, receipt env has none (D 570); P4.12.
26. Refusals: no `/s/` 400 `missing_session`; wrong secret 403 `wrong_vm`; ungranted provider 403 `no_grant`; body over 32 MiB 413; VM A's secret on VM B's port 403 `wrong_vm` (N 215–222); P4.12.
27. `vm.grants.update` mid-body; assert old credential finishes, next uses new; update naming another `owner_user_id` or a session grant rejected before swap; non-current `vm_id` is `stale` (D 505, 572); P4.12.
28. Upstream 401 once, then 401 with tunnel closed; assert exactly one re-resolve, then 401 surfaced within a bound (D 565; G11); P4.12.
29. `git push` from a dispatcher-launched process through `/git/<host>/`; assert success, no token in process env, token seen by the fake service (D 566); P4.12.
30. `GET /_g5/status` from a second VM without the secret; assert 403 (N 201; G10); P4.12.

Fleet (D; `reconcile_once(now)` after [stale_host.rs](../backend/g5-api-core/src/cloud/stale_host.rs) line 190):
31. Three missed heartbeats; assert `unreachable`, `place()` skips the host, incarnations current until `lease_expires_at`, then `failed` with `vm_id`, `host_id` null; hello with inventory restores `ready` (D 479); P4.13.
32. Occupancy equals `count(workspace_vms where status in (starting, running, stopping))`; crashed `starting` row released by the reconciler; P4.13.
33. Placement order retained root, region, `no_capacity` with `queued_for_capacity` position; quota refused with zero `ctl` commands on the `ScriptedHostClient` (D 643); P4.13.
34. `slot_unavailable` re-places elsewhere; `spec_rejected(unsupported_spec_version)` exclusion survives a `TestApp` restart (G8); P4.13.
35. Drain route returns a drain id, marks `draining`, one `host.drain` on retry; `DELETE` refused while running unless `force=true`, which records loss per VM (D 512); P4.13.
36. `api_rollouts.rs`: flush failure retried to the deadline, then `vm.stop`, `unflushed_loss_at` per VM, `host.drained`, termination; resume on the new image is a new attempt of the same logical task; old native requests invalidated (D 647; P 1198); P8.7.

Real hypervisor (E):
37. Boot from `VmSpec` to `ready`, `fs.read` over relayed `rpc`, `vm.stop` frees the slot; unknown `vm_id` in inventory kills the real VM within grace; P4.4b.
38. In-guest `curl http://g5-llm:<port>/_g5/status` with and without the secret; assert 200 and 403 `wrong_vm`; pins the shared-loopback door (N 363; G17); P4.12.
39. Warm resume reuses the retained root; cold resume after eviction yields an identical tree; record the three timings (D 546, 1083); P4.4b.

Stack (F):
40. `docker pause` 35 s then unpause; assert `unreachable` then `ready`, VM current, no replacement; `docker network disconnect` past TTL; assert `lease_expired_killed` replayed, row `failed`, replacement only after expiry; P4.4b.
41. `docker kill -s KILL g5-api` with a VM running; assert hello rebuilds the registry ([preview_tunnel.rs](../backend/g5-api-core/src/ws/preview_tunnel.rs) line 101), journal replays, rows unchanged (N 322); P4.4a.
42. Previous-release host connects, negotiates the previous version, boots a VM (a `ctl_conformance.rs` case with the previous fixture directory); the QA rehearsal per `docs/rollout.md` with observed `worker_hosts`, `workspace_vms`, `drains`, `rollouts` rows (P 1197) is process/manual until `scripts/qa-smoke.sh` exists; P8.7.

#### Model-based and property tests

- Lease bound (P4.4b): proptest over `{renew_sent(seq, t_depart), reply(seq, t_arrive, ttl), partition, drop_reply}` with random delay and reorder; invariant `host_deadline(t) <= cp_grant(t)`, boot grant `ttl + start_deadline`; the arm-from-arrival mutant must fail and is recorded in the PR.
- VmSpec validator (P4.4b): random subset of the eight defect classes; answer is the earliest in the order of D 540; any `..` or absolute `root_path` is `invalid_path`.
- Event journal (P4.4a): reference `(Vec<Event>, acked_prefix)` vs the real journal under `{emit, ack, reconnect, crash_restart}`; replay set is exactly `seq > acked`; `seq` monotone per `vm_id`.
- Idempotent command table (P4.4a): `{issue, complete, retry(t), expire_retention(t)}`; same key same result while retained, `not_found` after, never a second execution.
- Current-incarnation set (P4.4b, P4.12): `{accept_start, heartbeat_ack(set, gen), hello_accept(set)}`; a started `vm_id` stays current until a newer-generation set excludes it (pins G3).
- Host state machine (P4.13): only the transitions of N 26–30; `unreachable` never receives placement.
- Placement (P4.13): random fleets with derived occupancy; retained root, region, `no_capacity`; quota before send; a host answering `slot_unavailable` is not re-chosen for the same request.
- Proxy refusal table (P4.12): exhaustive over secret x incarnation x session x grant x body x client `Authorization`; exact status per N 215–222, precedence `wrong_vm`, `stale_incarnation`, `missing_session`, `no_grant`, `body_too_large`; client `Authorization` forwarded untouched.
- Grant swap (P4.12): two concurrent requests, one update; each recorded upstream header carries exactly one tagged credential.
- Protocol compatibility (P8.7): every corpus version decodes; every current enum variant has a fixture, enforced by an exhaustive `match`.

#### Boundary tests

Format: driven; boundary; assertion; ref; PR.

State machine

1. Every `legal_arcs()` row via routes; API plus Postgres; `tasks.status`, waiting label, `task_attempts.reason`, `provider_requests.status` match, every `forbidden()` row returns its 409; design 690–701; P4.6.
2. proptest event sequences; pure model; at most one attempt `running`, attempt increments only on a fresh execution, never on a same-exec_id retry; design 732; P4.6.
3. Sequence with `owner_cancel`; pure model; later events leave `cancelled`, no invoke/ensure/continuation intent; design 711; P4.6.
4. Attempt-1 C3 and ack after attempt 2 started, duplicate and shuffled seq; API; history rows with `mutates_current=false`, insert-ignore, `acked_event_seq` advances only contiguously; design 735; P4.6.
5. Continue and Cancel via `tokio::join`, 200 iterations; API; never attempt 2 `running` after `cancelled`; design 711, AAS 318; P4.6.
6. Policy change before Continue; API; new `policy_snapshot_ref` for attempt 2, attempt 1 unchanged; Codex `approval_policy=on-request` rejected at creation with a capability code; workflow origin never yields bypassPermissions; design 712, 803; P4.6, P4.8.

Dispatch

7. `modules.invoke` twice, one exec_id; socket; `started` then `already_running`, one receipt; AAS 133; P4.11.
8. Cancel, prune journal, delayed invoke; socket; `cancelled`, no receipt, no child; design 734; P4.11.
9. `G5_TEST_FAULT=before_invoke`, restart API; API plus socket; re-send with same exec_id on the current incarnation, one receipt, attempt 1, `start_confirmed=true`; design 720–722; P4.6.
10. `G5_TEST_FAULT=drop_invoke_reply`; reconcile; `already_running|already_finished(outcome)`, outcome applied once; design 733; P4.6.
11. `kill -9 g5d dispatch` mid-turn, restart; socket; `events.replay(from_seq)` ordered, live events behind, `outcomes.pending` lists the unacked C3, duplicate ack no-op, ack past the prefix refused; AAS 147; P4.11.
12. `G5_TEST_FAULT=after_c3_before_ack`, restart; API; second application no-op, `tasks.outcome`/`ended_at` set once, dependents released once; design 736; P4.6.
13. Input with argv or host path, and two executions in one VM; socket; schema rejection before spawn; independent seq spaces, reply for A never reaches B; design 1070; P4.11.

Adapter

14. Claude fake via the real launcher; child pipes; receipt `stdin_messages == [user frame]`, `stdin_closed_at == after_result`, `input.accepted` journaled after the write; design 768, plan 866; P4.11.
15. Recorded native question, then `input.respond`; adapter; `provider_request.opened(kind=question)`, exactly one `control_response` with recorded input plus answers; permission fixtures give allow/deny and `tool.completed` ok/declined; AAS 406; P4.11.
16. Fixture prefix cut at a random frame, EOF, exit in {0,1,130,137}; adapter; `succeeded` only with result frame, no pending request, exit 0; malformed result is `failed(protocol)`; unknown frames are notices; design 831; P4.11.
17. `control_cancel_request`, or exit with a request pending; adapter; `invalidated(provider_cancelled|process_exited)`, one outcome, later reply `request_gone` with no stdin write; design 817; P4.11.
18. `ignore_sigint {for_ms:20000}` then late success frame; adapter; SIGKILL at 15 s, one `suspended|cancelled` outcome, late frame is history; design 709; P4.11.
19. Codex fake; adapter; prompt plus EOF, `thread_started` binds session, `turn_completed` plus EOF plus exit 0 is the only success; interactive policy refused before spawn (no receipt); prose question opens no request; no `--disallowedTools` in argv, no ask_user in MCP config; design 807, AAS 433; P4.11.
20. Strict-resume fake refuses the restore; adapter; fresh session with delivered history, new `provider_session_id`, no unconsumed reply in the prompt; harvest with an older epoch rejected, `session_manifest_generation` unchanged (extends [session_files.rs](../backend/g5d-worker/src/runner/session_files.rs) 1101); design 823; P4.11.

Inbox

22. Second send during running and during held waiting; API; row stays `queued`, no second task; after terminal the next input creates a new task id in the same session; design 706; P4.7.
23. Edit and withdraw while `queued`, edit after `reserved`; API; 200 then 409, withdrawn row audit only; design 768; P4.7.
24. Fake exits before reading stdin; API; reservation back to `queued` with the same revision, no `delivered` or `delivery_unknown`; design 768; P4.7.
25. Fake writes receipt then blocks, `kill -9 g5d dispatch` before ack; API; `delivery_unknown`; journaled `input.accepted` gives `delivered` without resend; otherwise stays unknown, counted in `workspace_activity`, and Continue creates attempt 2 (`reason=vm_lost`) whose receipt has no re-sent prompt; AAS 284; P4.6, P4.7.
26. Typed send and templated launch of one text; API; identical `chat_messages`/`task_events` shapes, only `input_origin`/`template_ref` differ; design 1075; P4.8.

Native requests

27. Matrix {answer, deadline, cancel, exit, control_cancel, transfer} in every pairwise order; API plus receipt; one `task_attempts` row per attempt, `provider_requests` terminal once, at most one `control_response`, no task created, no `vm.start`; design 819; P4.6.

29. Same-version duplicate, different body, attempt-1 id after Continue, superseded epoch; `modules.input.respond`; `already_delivered`, 409, `request_gone`, `stale_execution`; reader and non-owning admin 403, receipt empty; AAS 158; P4.6.
30. Transfer mid-question; API; request invalidated, task held, dispatch blocked until new-owner Resume, attempt 2 on the new owner binding, old-owner reply 403 writes nothing; design 709, plan 750; P4.7.
31. Legacy `task_questions` rows; API; read-only, answer route 410 ([api_task_questions.rs](../backend/g5-api/tests/api_task_questions.rs) retargeted); P4.6.

Dependencies and publication

32. Job {A→B} plus jobless C, A held in turn by live question, deadline suspension, stop, transfer, drain authorization, retryable vm_lost, publication pending, cancel; API; B `queued`, job unfinished, A keeps id/session/job_id/edges, B dispatches once after final success (publication committed for the producer), cancel/failure follow the job policy; design 728, plan 769; P4.8.
33. `G5_TEST_FAULT=publication_fail_once`; API; attempt `succeeded`, task `waiting(publication)`, dependents held, reconciler publishes once, retry idempotent; design 440, plan 711; P4.6.
34. Drain authorization then owner stop before release; API; no requeue, held for Continue; design 640; P4.6.

Guest (microvm-agent; each has an API twin above)

35. Two sessions in one VM, third message mid-turn; guest; two receipts, third delivered after the first C3; plan 752; P4.7.
36. Stop mid-turn, Continue; guest; `chat_session_files` sha256 set equals harvested `/home/agent/.claude`, attempt-2 receipt shows `--resume`, old request id returns 409 `request_gone`; design 823; P4.7.
37. `docker pause` host past lease TTL after handoff before ack, unpause; guest plus API; `reason=vm_lost`, `delivery_unknown`, no `vm.start` until Resume, late harvest rejected by epoch; design 548; P4.7.
38. Restart g5-api while the fake blocks on a request; guest; card rehydrated, no spawn, answer reaches the same process, one receipt; design 738; P4.7.
39. Host drain with recorded authorization during A; guest; A continues as attempt 2 on the new image, B dispatches once; design 1077; P4.8.
40. Test 20 inside a real VM in g5-ci-rust `isolation-backends`; guest; same assertions; P4.11.

Browser

41. Queued strip edit/withdraw until reserved; card answer disabled after timeout with Continue offered; held task without running indicator; reader sees history without send/answer/cancel; Codex prose question shows no card; Playwright on the real API with fakes; design 702; P4.7, P4.10.

#### Model-based and property tests

- State x Event table: 6 states times 19 events (`invoke_intent`, `start_confirmed`, four dispositions, request opened/settled, `c3`, continue, resume, cancel, stop, transfer, `vm_lost`, `drain_authorized`, `deadline_expired`, publication committed/failed, `old_attempt_event`, `dependency_satisfied`); accepted pairs equal `legal_arcs()`, every other pair returns its §7.2 sentence's refusal code (design 705–712).
- One live attempt; attempt increments iff a fresh execution is created (design 732).
- After `owner_cancel`, status stays `cancelled`, no side-effect intent (design 711).
- `dependency_satisfied(A)` at most once, only when A `succeeded` and, for the producer, `publication_committed` (design 705, 728).
- `provider_requests` lattice `pending -> reply_pending -> responded|invalidated` monotone; any interleaving of {answer, deadline, cancel, exit, transfer} yields one terminal status and one C3 (design 819).
- Inbox lattice `queued -> reserved -> delivered|delivery_unknown -> delivered|released`; withdrawn only from `queued`; delivered rows immutable; one reservation per attempt (design 768).
- Job unfinished while any child is nonterminal or has a pending continuation; succeeded iff all children finally succeeded (design 705).
- `policy_snapshot_ref` write-once per (task_id, attempt) (design 803).
- Old-attempt events have `mutates_current=false` for every kind including C3 and acks (design 735).
- Adapter: any fixture prefix and exit code gives `succeeded` only with result frame, no pending request, exit 0 (test 16); k `native_request` steps under any answer schedule give exactly k ordered distinct `control_responses` or a matching count of `invalidated` events.

#### Gaps in design §15 and the plan

| Gap | Where | Recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| Response deadline default and test clock undefined | design 819, 1124; plan 717 | Name the default in §8.4; P4.6 ships a test-only override; require test 28 |
| Recording gate names no driver, files or CI check; record.sh cannot answer a control request | design 831; plan 863 | P4.11 Do 3 names `record-driver.py`, `native-*` files, `recording.json` entries and test 21; add stdin-closed-early, unanswered and wrong-id scenarios |
| "Agree the layered strategy" has no owner; P4.2b/P4.5 do not deliver the dispatcher harness P4.6/P4.11 need | plan 27, 557, 661 | `spawn_test_dispatcher()` in P4.2b acceptance, facade binding in P4.5; check this document into docs/ |

| C6 shutdown flush fails | §5.8 line 413 | test 26 | `blocked`; retry releases |

| M1–M7 merge | §10.6 lines 932–939 | tests 33–38 | per test |

#### Model-based and property tests

- Result, publication and cleanup machine (`proptest`, g5-api-core, 1000 cases, recorded seed): state = task status, attempt, candidates by key, selection, request status, result, `cleanup_status`, purpose, generation; events = submit, continue, c3(ok|fail|interrupted), publish_tick(ok|fail), cleanup_tick(ok|flush_fail|stale_generation), cancel, keep_as_workspace, idle_tick(payload_external), delete_child. Invariants: one result per request; the result is the selected candidate of the attempt whose C3 succeeded; no attempt < current selected or published after continue; completed implies succeeded and a result; dependents released iff succeeded; cleanup only for review|explainer, completed, no unfinished work, no live request, matching generation; keep never changes request or result; idle stops only when payload external or nothing pending; cancel after a result refused (§7.2 lines 698–703; P4.6 item 8 line 720).
- Merge machine (`proptest`): states none, pending, provider_merged, applied, failed; events pin, authorize_fail, mergeability_fail, provider_ok, provider_lost_reply, provider_head_moved, import_ok, import_fail, local_tx_ok, local_cas_fail, receipt_found, receipt_missing_target_moved, crash, retry. Invariants: SHAs constant per op id; failed only from pending with nothing applied; provider_merged reaches only applied; receipt implies applied; retry never mints a second op id; local path never calls the provider (line 937).
- Authorization table, exhaustive: actor (parent owner, parent exec, child owner, child exec, parent reader, non-owning admin, removed reader, outsider, stale exec) × parent access × route (submit, get, list, reply) × tombstoned; one table drives test and docs (line 448).
- Cleanup predicate and idle policy tables over all inputs (unfinished work, live request, queued input, payload external, completed, generation match, terminal or preview activity); queued input held, never dropped; terminal or preview activity never vetoes `purpose_complete` (lines 456–458).
- Idempotency-key algebra: per attempt, candidates = distinct keys with first digest; other digests conflict; across attempts the same key is distinct.
- Source-manifest digest is a pure function of the sorted `(repo_id, ref, oid)` vector; permutation preserves, one OID change alters; PR manifests carry no receipt id (line 444).
- Restricted Markdown fuzz (`fast-check`): random HTML, widget fences, schemes, Mermaid `click` yield no `iframe`, `script` or `on*` and only http(s), mailto or relative hrefs; the default profile still mounts the frame ([MarkdownRenderer.tsx](../frontend/src/components/shared/MarkdownRenderer.tsx) line 419).

### Frontend, end-to-end flows and CI

Scope: the capability table ([design §5.1](lean-backend-design-v9.md) lines 255–264), read-only editors and explicit Save (§9.2–§9.3 lines 862–872), preview (§6.8 lines 619–633), terminal (§6.9 line 637), transfer and checked fork (§5.8 lines 396–434), results (§5.9 lines 440–458), search removal (§9.5 lines 879–885). The Playwright fixtures that spawn a legacy worker over `/api/internal` ([provided-agents-fixture.ts](../frontend/e2e/provided-agents-fixture.ts), [synthetic-worker.ts](../frontend/e2e/fixtures/synthetic-worker.ts)) die with P4.9a/P4.9b, and the browser lane requires KVM fatally ([cloudbuild-ci-e2e.yaml](../infra/ci/cloudbuild-ci-e2e.yaml) lines 1627–1630) while `just ci-docker` on macOS has none. Every browser test below therefore assumes a `process` VM backend in `g5d host` that spawns `g5d dispatch` (runnable against a directory, [plan P4.2a](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 530) as a child and dials it on loopback, with facade, fence, proxies and `vm.stop` unchanged. Read-only is proven by `aria-disabled` plus an `aria-describedby` reason, zero mutation requests in the network log, zero facade audit rows and an axe pass.

| Name | Replaces | Exists today or new | Fidelity risk | PR that must land it |

| `g5d host --vm-backend process` | libkrun, virtio-fs, passt, uid 1000 | new | no OOM or VMM-exit loss path; authorization paths identical; substrate proven only in the KVM lane | P4.4b (pulled from P9.1 item 3) |
| `frontend/e2e/fixtures/workspace-vm.ts`: `forkWorkspace`, `resumeAndWaitReady`, `stop`, `transfer`, `asSecondUser`, `readFacadeAudit`, `hostJournal` | the two legacy fixtures; `createWorkspaceClone` (38 spec files) | new; `authedApi` ([helpers.ts](../frontend/e2e/helpers.ts) line 460), `setOAuthStubIdentity` ([oauth.ts](../frontend/e2e/oauth.ts) line 91), `setSecrets` (helpers.ts line 1592) exist | DB reads over `G5_E2E_DATABASE_URL` (yaml line 2633) are an oracle only; a vitest grep forbids `/api/internal` in `frontend/e2e` | P4.5 |
| oauth-stub identities owner, org member, invited reader, non-owning admin, removed member, outsider ([server.js](../infra/docker/oauth-stub/server.js), [seed-ci-org.sh](../scripts/seed-ci-org.sh)); stub `GET /repos/:full_name/branches` (200 or 503) | GitHub OAuth; provider lookup in fork step 2 | partial: two identities (oauth.ts lines 35–42); rest new | identity switch is global, so two-session tests log both in first; 503 is the only unavailability case | P4.5 |
| `runtime_facade_audit(workspace_id, vm_id, actor, method, outcome, at)` | a test-only counter | new (absent from docs and backend) | must be production data or the zero-VM-calls proof is test-only | P4.5 |
| seeded `.g5/preview_profiles/{echo-server,ws-echo,exit-3,write-tracked}.yaml`, `.g5/e2e/*.jsonl`; purpose-idle override 5 s; `G5D_HOST_MAX_MICROVMS=1` | a dev server; waiting 15–30 min; a second host | new except the slot cap ([justfile](../justfile) line 127); [seed-e2e-workspace.sh](../scripts/seed-e2e-workspace.sh) seeds no `.g5/`; override designed (§5.8 line 414), unimplemented | `ws-echo` covers the long-lived `http` stream; policy and placement paths are real | P5.1, P4.11, P4.13 |
| `terminal-transcript` aria-live mirror; `window.__g5Terminal` only when `import.meta.env.MODE==='e2e'` | reading the xterm canvas | new | assert mirror text and `buffer.active.length` together | P5.2 |
| `infra/ci/cloudbuild-ci-browser.yaml`, `frontend/e2e/quarantine.txt`, `retries: 0` for `@stateful` | eight `allowFailure` stages ([e2e-stage-results.tsv](../infra/ci/e2e-stage-results.tsv)), `--grep-invert` (yaml lines 3173, 3284), seed `trap ... exit 0` (line 2968), `retries: 2` ([playwright.config.ts](../frontend/playwright.config.ts) line 83) | new | advisory until green on three docs-only PRs; PRs attach a `just ci-docker 7` receipt meanwhile | P8.6a (new early split) |

#### Boundary tests

Capabilities and reads (§5.1 lines 255–264, §9.1 lines 847–853; P4.10 unless noted).

1. `capabilities.test.ts` and `backend/g5-api/tests/api_workspace_capabilities.rs`: each `capability-matrix.tsv` row equals `workspaceCapabilities(row)`, and each listed route called as that principal returns the row's status and `source`; `count(*) FROM runtime_facade_audit WHERE actor=$reader` is 0. P4.10, P4.5.
2. `sharing-matrix.spec.ts`: six principals × `org`/`invite_only`; assert controls, `data-source` badge, `readFacadeAudit(actor)=0`, outsider 404, removed member's watch socket closed on reload. §15 line 1080.
3. `CodeEditor.readonly.test.tsx`: reader gets Monaco `readOnly` and Save described by "Fork to edit"; unattached owner gets "Resume to edit" and `resume-workspace`; Ctrl+S records zero PUTs in msw. §9.2 line 864. P2.2.
4. `explicit-save.spec.ts`: Resume flips `data-vm-state=running` and badge `live`; Ctrl+S sends `expected_hash` and `expected_vm_id`; a comment anchored at (path, line, hash) survives Save. P2.2.
5. `workspace-vm.spec.ts`: scenario `write-file` writes `agent.txt`; Stop shows flush time and badge `flush` with OID; Resume restores it; Restart shows a new `vm_id`; zero PUTs while stopped; no `GET /files` in a 10 s window once `refetchInterval` ([useFiles.ts](../frontend/src/hooks/useFiles.ts) line 36) is gone. §5.8 lines 384–386.
6. `changes-and-push.spec.ts`: Push with a dirty tree and no local commits leaves the branch OID unchanged (`GET /repos/:id/branches`); no Commit control. §9.2 line 866.

Fork and transfer (§5.8 lines 398–403, 412, 434; P4.5 lands, P4.10 extends).

7. `fork-dialog.spec.ts`: `feature/x` byte-identical in two code repos and docs; reload during a 5 s injected ref delay shows "creating" with no Start, then stopped with `owner_resume_required=true`; collision and 503 cases in the fault table. §15 line 1063.
8. `transfer.spec.ts`: admin transfers a running workspace while the old owner holds a dirty editor, terminal and preview; the old tab turns read-only within one poll, keeps the text with a copy-or-fork notice, issues no write; `workspace_ownership_transfers` has from/to/requester/completed_at; new owner resumes with the flushed edit; preview task `cancelled(transfer)`, not restarted. §6.8 line 627.
9. Rust e2e `reader_fork_zero_source_calls`: dirty running source; child OIDs equal the receipt vector; no audit rows for the source `vm_id`. §15 line 1080.

Preview (§6.8 lines 623–631, [workspace-vm-api §3.1](workspace-vm-api-v9.md) lines 90–100; P5.1).

10. `api_preview_task.rs`: reader POST gives 403; stopped owner gives `WORKSPACE_NOT_RUNNING` and no `vm.start` in the host journal; compound start records `queued` before capacity; owner ticket from another session gives 403 with zero relayed bytes.
11. `preview.spec.ts`: stopped owner button reads "Start workspace and preview"; queue shows `preview` `queued` before attachment; iframe loads `echo-server`; queue Cancel and panel Stop settle the same task; reader sees history and "Fork to preview" only; `write-tracked` puts README.md in Changes and in the stopped receipt view. §15 line 1081.
12. Rust e2e `preview_service_exit_no_restart`: `exit-3` gives task `failed`, no retry in 30 s, VM `running`.

Terminal (§6.9 line 637, [plan P5.2](lean-implementation-plan-v9.md) line 976; P5.2).

13. `api_terminal_ws.rs`: upgrade as reader or non-owning admin gives 403 before any `pty` stream; owner echo; `last_activity_at` advances after a keystroke.
14. `terminal.spec.ts`: `echo g5-$RANDOM` appears in `terminal-transcript` and `__g5Terminal.buffer`; resize reaches the dispatcher log; session survives editor navigation; reader has no tab; base workspace offers "Fork and open a terminal" through the shared dialog.

Review loop (§5.9 lines 450–456, §15 line 1082; P4.14).

15. `api_workspace_requests.rs`: reader requests and accepts, owns a stopped child pinned to the source OID; `g5d pr result submit` from a non-producer exec gives 403; candidate then final C3 yields one `workspace_results` row.
16. `review-loop.spec.ts`: accept, "Start review", scenario `submit-result-then-continue`; parent shows `running` after submit, then `completed` with sanitized Markdown, findings and a reply; child `released`.

Removal (§9.5 line 881; P8.9, P2.2).

17. `router_inventory.rs`: no `/search` path except `/workspaces/:id/sessions/search`, no `/commit`, no `/flush`; the snapshot diff equals the documented removal list.
18. `search-removed.spec.ts` and `action-registry.test.ts`: no SearchBar docked, mobile or detached; Ctrl+K opens nothing search-related; `GET /api/v1/workspaces/:id/search` gives 404; chat-history search finds a seeded message; Monaco Find works; no `search.*`, `commit.*`, `flush.*` action ids.
19. Vitest greps: no `yjs|y-websocket|y-monaco|Y\.Doc` in `frontend/src` (P2.2); no component derives controls from `created_by` or invites (P4.10); `frontend/e2e` never calls `/api/internal` (P4.5).

#### Fault-injection points

| Point | Design ref | Mechanism to inject | Expected observation |
|---|---|---|---|
| Stop between editor load and Save | §9.2 line 864; §5.8 line 427 | owner loads editor, `POST /stop` via `authedApi`, Ctrl+S; Rust e2e times the PUT inside the admission-close window | `WORKSPACE_STOPPING`; no `vm.start`; text kept with "Resume to save"; receipt excludes it |
| Stale incarnation on Save | §9.2 line 864; §6.4 line 552 | second context stops and resumes; first tab saves with cached `expected_vm_id` | 409 stale incarnation; editor reloads before any further mutation |
| Agent write races an edit | §9.2 line 866 | `write-file` with `delay_ms` on the open file | "changed on disk"; Save 409 with current content; Overwrite sends the new hash; dialog owner-only |
| Transfer flush failure | §5.8 line 412 | reflog-obstruct the flush ref write (the landing-test technique) | `aborted`; old owner kept; VM up; Save works again; one audit row on retry |
| Two preview starts, cancel before invoke, delayed event | §6.8 line 625 | two POSTs within 50 ms; cancel while `queued_for_capacity` (slot cap 1 occupied), then free the slot; replay `port.opened` with the superseded attempt id | one `running`, loser `cancelled` with replacement disposition and exited first; cancelled start never attaches; stale port absent from Ports |
| Copied ticket, guessed port | §6.8 line 631 | copy the ticket cookie into the reader context; request the relay and a guessed port | 403, zero relayed bytes, no reader audit row, `last_activity_at` unchanged |
| Delayed terminal write after transfer | §6.9 line 637; P5.2 line 976 | Rust e2e writes on the old stream 1 s after commit | old socket closed typed; write refused `stale`; new owner unaffected |
| Fork collision in docs or at provider | §5.8 lines 398–400 | seed `refs/heads/feature/x` in the docs bare repo; stub returns the name or 503 | 409 naming the repo, zero refs, dialog open; 503 retryable with the same idempotency key; no suffix |
| Candidate submitted, then question or failure | §5.9 line 450 | scenarios `submit-result-then-question`, `-then-fail` | question: request `running`, owner answers, then publishes; fail: no `workspace_results` row, request `failed`, cleanup untouched, candidate labelled unpublished for the child owner |
| Cleanup blocked after publication | §5.9 line 456 | obstruct the child's flush ref before the `purpose_complete` stop | result visible; `cleanup_status=blocked` with Retry; retry gives `released`; nothing deleted |
| Idle abandonment of a child | §5.9 line 458 | purpose-idle override 5 s | child stops `idle`; request stays `running`; Resume for the child owner only |
| Reader wake paths | §5.1 lines 255–264; §5.8 line 436 | reader: chat, Preview, terminal, PUT, smart-HTTP push; removed member: reload | all refused; `workspace_vms.status` unchanged; no `vm.start`; no reader audit rows |

#### Model-based and property tests

- Capability matrix (fast-check in vitest, proptest in g5-api, one TSV): roles × `{stopped, starting, running, stopping, failed, queued_for_capacity}` × admission × `{is_base, purpose, owner_resume_required}`; no reader tuple yields an enabled write or execute capability; Rust asserts the route decision. §5.1 lines 255–264.
- Preview lifecycle (proptest on the real TaskService with a dispatcher double at `modules.invoke`): at most one preview in `{queued, starting, running}`; terminal states absorb; a non-current (task, attempt, vm_id) event is a no-op; duplicate keys return the original; no restart after stop, loss or transfer. §6.8 lines 625–627.
- Editor Save store (fast-check): dirty text is discarded only by explicit Reload, Overwrite or discard; no PUT unless owner, running and admission open. §9.2–§9.3.
- Fork name validation (proptest): `validate_workspace_name(s)` returns exactly `s` or an error and agrees with `git check-ref-format --branch`; for accepted `n`, `n/x` and any slash-containing prefix conflict in the reservation checker. §5.8 line 398.
- Read-source resolver: `live` only for the attached owner with admission open, `flush` only with a complete receipt, OID vector never mixed. §9.1 lines 847–855.
- Result sanitizer: random payloads with `<script>`, `on*`, `javascript:` and `data:` render none of them. §5.9 line 446.
- Route inventory snapshot: P8.9 and P2.2 remove lines only. §9.5 line 881.

| "Zero VM calls" has no mechanism | §15 line 1080; P4.5 line 673 | `runtime_facade_audit` as production data in P4.5, named in §6.7 |
| No `g5d host` in the test stack; docker-ci starts `g5-test-worker-microvm`; fixtures spawn legacy workers | justfile line 908; P4.9b line 804 | P4.5 owns the compose/docker-ci host service and `workspace-vm.ts`; P4.9b deletes both legacy fixtures |
| Six principals required, two seeded | oauth.ts lines 35–42; P4.10 line 847 | four identities in P4.5; P4.10 names them |
| P5.1 "retains" preview Playwright coverage that does not exist | P5.1 line 960; no `preview*.spec.ts` in [flows](../frontend/e2e/flows) | "add preview.spec.ts"; §15 line 1081 names cancel-during-attachment |
| Terminal output is not DOM-observable and has no screen-reader transcript | P5.2 lines 979–980 | require the mirror and the e2e buffer handle; add "terminal passes axe" to §15 |
| No read-only assertion standard | §15 line 1074; P2.2 line 413; P4.10 line 834 | add the scope-paragraph sentence to §15 and the TSV oracle to P4.10 |

| `retries: 2` reruns lifecycle specs on mutated workspaces | playwright.config.ts line 83 | `retries: 0` for `@stateful`; one fork per test; gate on [report-flaky-tests.js](../scripts/report-flaky-tests.js) |
| Browser stages untrusted until wave 13 | plan §5.4 line 332; §2 item 11 line 31 | P8.6a before P2.2, P4.10 and P8.9; amend §2 item 11 |

| No v9 value-loop rows | [value_loop_matrix.tsv](../tests/e2e/value_loop_matrix.tsv) (VL-01 to VL-17 cite CRDT drafts and worker claims) | reserve VL-20 edit/save/stop/resume, VL-21 reader fork, VL-22 review result, VL-23 transfer, VL-24 preview, VL-25 terminal, VL-26 search removal |
| §7.1 Fork and Review rows list backend PRs only | plan lines 1358–1359 | add P4.10 to Fork; add P4.14's UI observation to Review |
